# Test map: 0.3 and 0.4.0

**0.4.0:** see [Part 1b](#part-1b-the-0400-changelog) for the whole
[0.4.0] section, item by item (audit of 2026-11-02).

Each user-facing change in CHANGELOG.md's **[0.2.2] - Unreleased** section
(shipping as 0.3), including the Fixed entries, matched to the tests that
check it. Use it to find the test to run, or to extend, when you change a
feature. It was made in the overnight test audit of 2026-10-25. That audit
also closed the high-risk gaps it found; the tests it added are shown in
**bold** with *(new)*.

The map has two parts:

- **Part 1** is organised by changelog entry. It covers what ships in 0.3.
- **[Part 2](#part-2-earlier-features-by-area)** is organised by area. It
  covers features from before 0.2.2 and comes from the 0.2.x audit of
  2026-10-22, which was TEST_COVERAGE.md. Its statuses date from that audit.
  The bugs it found (B1–B9) are fixed, and their regression tests are listed
  in Part 1's Fixed sections.

## Keeping it current

When a feature lands, add its rows to Part 1 (or to Part 2 if it
changes an earlier feature).

- **When a changelog entry is added**, add a row for each separate claim it
  makes. The claims a test can check: what is output on the wire, what is
  saved, what undo does, what a widget shows.
- **When you add a test**, put `Suite::function` in its row. Use the test
  class name and the private slot from the test's `*_test.h`.
- **When you rename or remove a test**, fix its rows. Check with
  `grep -rn "Suite::function" TEST_MAP.md`.
- Row IDs (M001…) are only for referring to rows. Don't renumber them; give
  a new row the next number.

**Status:**

- **covered:** a test drives the behaviour the way a user does (real
  widgets, actions, playback ticks, save and load) and asserts the result.
- **partial:** the test asserts only part of the behaviour, only its
  engine half, or only a getter.
- **none:** no test reaches it.

Tests that run only with an environment variable set (`COURSE_RIG`,
`OFFICE_*`, `FM_DH_SHOW`, `QLC_TRACKBACK_SHOW` and the screenshot switches)
are not counted. A row marked **(bug)** has a test with QEXPECT_FAIL: the
test asserts what the changelog promises, and that check fails today.

**Risk:**

- **high:** safety, DMX output, playback, saving and loading, undo, crashes.
- **med:** a feature a show relies on.
- **low:** a UI convenience or a label.

**Running a row's test:** `QLC_TEST_ONLY="ui/test/<dir>" ./check-all.sh --quick`.
To run one function, go to the build dir and run the program with the
function name as its argument. Set `QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen` and add
`-platform offscreen`. A test's `test.sh` does not set offscreen itself.

## What the 2026-10-25 audit found

The audit's bugs each had a test asserting what the changelog promises,
with the failing check marked `QEXPECT_FAIL`. All three, and three of the
findings below, were fixed the same night (CHANGELOG 0.3.0, Fixed); their
checks now pass, with no `QEXPECT_FAIL` left:

- **Not-ours channels could still be patched by two paths (M015). Fixed.**
  `Doc::addFixture()` with an address and a patched fixture's move
  (`Fixture::setAddress()`, through `Doc::slotFixtureChanged`) now refuse
  not-ours channels, through the same `Doc::isOnNotOurs()` that
  `canPatchFixtures()` uses; the Add Fixture dialog marks the address taken.
  Test: `VenueRig_Test::patchingOntoNotOursIsRefusedEveryWay`.
- **Build Delete → Undo garbled a Show (M438). Fixed.** The undo snapshot
  and restore use `Show::copyExactlyFrom()` (same tracks and items, no
  "Copy of" functions) instead of `Show::copyFrom()`. Tests:
  `ProgrammingManager_Test::undoingADeletePutsItBackOnItsShowTrack` (both
  with the Shows tab opened and never opened), `Show_Test::copyExactly`.
- **The ColorSource CYC definition had no beam angle (M292). Fixed.** ETC
  publishes none ("N/A (asymmetrical)"); 100° is derived from its
  photometrics and noted in the file. Test:
  `FixtureDefCheck_Test::theEtcColorSourceDefinitionsLoadClean`.

Findings that are not marked as bugs:

- **The venue file kept only what this build knows. Fixed:** elements a
  newer build wrote (between and inside venues, inside `<House>` and
  `<Practice>`) are kept through the next save. Unknown attributes on known
  elements are still dropped.
- **A clash found when a venue opens left only an overlap unpatched. Fixed
  (decision, following Q138):** a house light that clashes in any way
  (overlap, not ours, a Tour slot) goes in unpatched, keeping its address.
  A tour light that clashes stays patched as the show has it.
- **A practice can add a `<FixtureRig>` entry to the show file.** It holds
  default values, for a scrambled light that had no entry. The calibration
  is unchanged.
- **If ShowManager had never been shown, Build Delete left a dead Show
  item. Fixed:** `ShowManager::slotFunctionRemoved` no longer returns early
  while its shows list is empty.
- **Two hold tests depended on machine load.** They counted wall-clock time
  per joystick tick, so a loaded machine could save early. They now tick
  at the nominal 20 ms:
  `FocusCalls_Test::theControllerDoesItThroughTheProgrammer` and
  `FocusSession_Test::theControllerReachesItThroughTheProgrammer`.
- **A text element can't take a child element.** A newer build that put an
  element inside a chaser's or collection's `<Step>id</Step>` would stop
  this build's reader there. Everything else in the show reads past what it
  doesn't know (`ShowFiles_Test::aShowFromANewerBuildOpens`).

| M984 | Nothing laid over anything | Every app tab (Connections, Fixtures, Lighting Studio, Functions, Build with and without a scene, Show Tree with a cue and with a layer, Shows, Virtual Console, Simple Desk, Run), the Workbench with every Look Editor page (colour, colour with a wheel + ring fixture, dimmer, aim, pan/tilt, shutter, strobe, gobo, zoom, beam, effect, formation) and a scene, the stock function editors, the panels and ~45 dialogs, each at its minimum, 1280x800 and 1920x1080, offscreen: no two visible controls overlap, none is cut off by its window outside a scroll area, no label or button is narrower or shorter than its text, none is squeezed to zero | **LayoutSanity_Test::everyScreenIsLaidOutCleanly** (data-driven, one row per screen), **::theCheckerFindsWhatItShould** (the checker on hand-made overlaps, cut-offs and a squeezed label; a stack's hidden page, a scroll area's content and a marked overlay pass) (ui/test/layoutsanity) (new) | none | covered | med | Known issues are listed in the test's `knownIssues()` with a TODO ref (docs/project/TODO.md "Layout sanity"), reported but not failed; a row no longer hit is warned about. Word-wrapped labels are not checked for height. The Virtual Console's own widgets may overlap (the user places them). The app window's "min" is 1024x640. |
| M984b | Fits a 1024x640 laptop | Every dialog among the screens has a smallest size a 1024x640 laptop can show (less title and menu bar, 1024x590), so OK / Cancel are never under the screen; the Studio editors (Tower, Truss, Platform, Pipe) at 1024x590, 1280x800 and 1920x1080: fit, scroll their sides, OK / Cancel in view, nothing cut or laid over, the plot's edge labels clear of its measurements; every menu button with text (InstantPopup) wide enough for text plus arrow, and the app style draws the text clear of the arrow; Scene group looks keeps a look's name at its smallest; the Show Tree shows a layer's name at 1024 and an empty info column gives up its room | **LayoutSanity_Test::everyScreenIsLaidOutCleanly** ("off-screen dialog", "menu-arrow-over-text"), **::theStudioEditorsFitALaptop**, **::menuButtonsKeepTheirArrowOffTheText**, **::theSceneGroupLooksShowNamesAtTheSmallest**, **::theShowTreeShowsCueNamesWithFreshSettings** (ui/test/layoutsanity) (new) | none | covered | med | UX-34 / UX-36, first UX review findings. |

### Adding a screen to layoutsanity_test

One row in `screens()` in `ui/test/layoutsanity/layoutsanity_test.cpp`:
`screen("Name", [](App *app) -> QWidget * { return new MyDialog(docOf(app)); })`,
built the way the app builds it, on the test's show (`lesson-3-9-finish.qlcc`,
read-only; `docOf(app)`). A tab of the app window is `tab("Label")`, with an
optional second lambda that puts it in a state (select a node, open a
function). Return `nullptr` when the show has nothing to open it on (the row
is skipped). A widget drawn over others on purpose gets
`setProperty("layoutSanityOverlay", true)` with a one-line comment where it
is made. Run with `LAYOUT_SANITY_SHOTS=<dir>` to save what the test saw, one
PNG per screen and size, failures outlined in red. A failure you can't fix
now goes in `knownIssues()` with a TODO line, never silently.

## 0.4.0: Play and Solo on the Show Tree's pages (solo, 2026-11-01)

CHANGELOG **[0.4.0] - Unreleased**, Fixed, "Play and Solo on the Show Tree's cue and layer pages".
**Regression guard:** these tests keep Play and Solo from disappearing again.
They were never on the tree's own cue and layer pages (only in the
Workbench docked there, from 4984db0be on 2026-10-22), so a cue opened by a
click had neither, and in Operate the docked Workbench hid Play (4984db0be,
updateOnRig). Both tests fail on 987c9c56e (before the fix) with "missing".

# Part 1b: the 0.4.0 changelog

CHANGELOG **[0.4.0] - Unreleased** (0.3.1 + 0.4 + most of 0.5), every
top-level entry, one row per separate claim. Made in the pre-beta coverage
audit of 2026-11-02 (tag tcov): each row's tests were read, not matched by
name. Rows M591–M663 above cover parts of the same section in more detail
and are not repeated here. Tests added by the audit are **bold** with *(new)*.
"n/a" rows are docs, course text, data or dev tooling.

## What the 2026-11-02 audit found

- **Deleting a function a playing Show runs crashed. Fixed.** ShowRunner
  kept the deleted Function* in its running queue and the next frame used
  it. It now notes removed ids from `Doc::functionRemoved` and drops them at
  the next write/stop (one atomic read per frame). Test:
  `Show_Test::aSceneDeletedWhileTheShowPlaysLeavesTheRestPlaying`.
- **(bug, QEXPECT_FAIL)** the engine `Show` still saves the block of a
  deleted function (only the Shows tab prunes it). Same test; TODO.md.
- **Question: "Design only" effect fades.** A plan (AI Assist) is refused in
  Operate, a typed `Steps effect Sinelon fade 2` is not (tree edits are
  allowed in Operate and reach the stage at the next GO). CHANGELOG wording
  or code; TODO.md.
- **Question: QLab out while Blind is on.** Previews and Blind edits send
  nothing (tested); a real GO with Blind on still sends. TODO.md.
- A running list cue keeps its old Formation output until its next GO; the
  Design preview follows at once (tested as is).
- **Question: Teaching's "Run this show for real?" from the web page.** A
  show loaded over web access or opening itself in Operate asks nothing
  (tested); the web page's Design/Operate toggle reaches
  `App::slotModeOperate` without `m_unattendedOperate`, so it could ask on
  the console. TODO.md.
- Adding a fixture the engine refuses (past universe 1024) shows no message
  in the Add Fixture flow (`FixtureCreator::addFromDialog`). TODO.md.
- The audit's tests add about 15 s of test-program time to a gate leg
  (largest: AppViews ~2 s, MainBar ~1.2 s, the web ones ~1 s each); run in
  parallel, under 10 s of wall time per leg.

# Part 2: earlier features, by area

In this part, "Before" and "After" are the 0.2.x audit's own before and
after. A test marked *(new)* was new in that audit. The bugs it found, B1
to B9, are all fixed. Their tests no longer use QEXPECT_FAIL.

## Part 2: not tested, and why

- **Command line (`-o`, `-p`, `-k`, `-w`; a bare file name is not opened).**
  `parseArgs()` lives in `main/main.cpp`, which no test links. The seam
  needed: move it into the ui library as
  `QLCArgs::parse(const QStringList &)`.
- **The autosave recovery prompt.** `App::checkAutosaveRecovery()` returns
  before asking on the offscreen and minimal platforms, whatever the
  environment says. What is tested: the copy is written, kept with nobody to
  ask, and removed by Save or Discard. The seam needed: a
  `QLC_RECOVERY_ANSWER=yes|no` override checked before the platform test.
  Removal of the copy at a clean quit (`~App`) would need a second App in
  the process.
- **Cloud storage Download Now / Skip.** These need a stand-in for an
  online-only file provider. Only the "noted, not waited on" startup path is
  tested.
- **HID hotplug and line numbering.** `plugins/hid` has no test directory
  and needs a stubbed device enumerator.
- **The Lighting Studio's rig undo has no redo,** so there is nothing to test
  there.
- **Audio and MIDI effects** need a fake spectrum / note source. **Power
  circuit Auto-assign** lives in `PowerDistributionWidget`; it would be
  testable once its bin-packing moves into the engine.
- **Low-risk UI conveniences** were left for after the release. These are the
  rows marked *none* or *partial* with risk *low* above: the grid editor's
  marquee and arrow picker, Library pins, ⌘A in the outline, Look Editor
  sub-pages, outline contrast, and the Rig Overview camera mouse controls.
